```
=== api/produce/route.ts ===
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'
export const fetchCache = 'force-no-store'

export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(request.url)
  const status = searchParams.get('status') ?? 'available'

  const { data: produce, error } = await supabase
    .from('produce_listings')
    .select('*')
    .eq('status', status)
    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })

  if (error) return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 500 })
  if (!produce?.length) return NextResponse.json([])

  const farmerIds = [...new Set(produce.map((p) => p.farmer_id))]

  const { data: farmers } = await supabase
    .from('farmers')
    .select('id, name, village, slug, phone, method, region_slug, pickup_locations, pickup_slots, lat, lng')
    .in('id', farmerIds)
    .eq('active', true)

  const farmerMap = Object.fromEntries((farmers ?? []).map((f) => [f.id, f]))

  const result = produce
    .map((p) => ({ ...p, farmer: farmerMap[p.farmer_id] ?? null }))
    .filter((p) => p.farmer !== null)

  return NextResponse.json(result)
}
```

```
-- ============================================================
-- RLS: allow deletes on produce_listings
-- Run this in the Supabase SQL editor (Dashboard → SQL Editor)
-- ============================================================
-- The farmer dashboard needs to delete a farmer's own produce.
-- Matches the public-write model used elsewhere in the MVP
-- (anon INSERT is already allowed). Tighten with auth.uid() once
-- farmer auth is wired up.

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "produce_listings public delete" ON produce_listings;
CREATE POLICY "produce_listings public delete"
  ON produce_listings FOR DELETE
  USING (true);
```

```
=== api/farmer/update-listing/route.ts ===
import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
import { getFarmerSessionFromRequest } from '@/lib/farmer-session'

export const runtime = 'nodejs'
export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'

const UUID_RE = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i

// Disallowed payload keys — never let the client mutate ownership, status
// flags, or audit columns through this generic update endpoint.
const FORBIDDEN_KEYS = new Set([
  'id', 'farmer_id', 'created_at', 'updated_at',
])

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  // farmerId now comes from the signed cookie, not the request body.
  const session = getFarmerSessionFromRequest(request)
  if (!session) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Please log in first.' }, { status: 401 })

  const supabaseAdmin = createClient(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,
  )

  const body = await request.json().catch(() => null)
  if (!body || typeof body !== 'object') {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid request' }, { status: 400 })
  }

  const { listingId, payload } = body as { listingId?: unknown; payload?: unknown }
  if (typeof listingId !== 'string' || !UUID_RE.test(listingId)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid listing id' }, { status: 400 })
  }
  if (!payload || typeof payload !== 'object' || Array.isArray(payload)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid payload' }, { status: 400 })
  }

  // Strip any forbidden keys before forwarding to the DB.
  const cleanPayload: Record<string, unknown> = {}
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(payload as Record<string, unknown>)) {
    if (!FORBIDDEN_KEYS.has(k)) cleanPayload[k] = v
  }

  // Verify the listing belongs to the logged-in farmer. We also pull the
  // current status + stock so we can keep the sold-out flag in sync below.
  const { data: existing } = await supabaseAdmin
    .from('produce_listings')
    .select('id, status, stock_qty')
    .eq('id', listingId)
    .eq('farmer_id', session.farmerId)
    .maybeSingle()

  if (!existing) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Listing not found or access denied' }, { status: 403 })
  }

  // Stock ↔ status sync. Whenever a farmer edits the quantity we refresh the
  // sold-out flag: raising stock above 0 clears a stuck "sold_out" so the
  // listing goes live again, and dropping stock to 0 marks it sold out. We only
  // ever flip between these two states, so moderator states (suspended,
  // rejected, pending_review) and "coming_soon" are never clobbered. If the
  // client explicitly sent a status we respect it and skip the auto-sync.
  if (!('status' in cleanPayload)) {
    const nextStock =
      'stock_qty' in cleanPayload
        ? (cleanPayload.stock_qty as number | null)
        : (existing.stock_qty as number | null)
    if (typeof nextStock === 'number') {
      if (nextStock > 0 && existing.status === 'sold_out') {
        cleanPayload.status = 'available'
      } else if (nextStock <= 0 && existing.status === 'available') {
        cleanPayload.status = 'sold_out'
      }
    }
  }

  const { data: updated, error } = await supabaseAdmin
    .from('produce_listings')
    .update(cleanPayload)
    .eq('id', listingId)
    .eq('farmer_id', session.farmerId)
    .select('id, status, stock_qty')

  if (error) return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 500 })
  if (!updated?.length) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Update failed' }, { status: 500 })

  // Return the resolved status/stock so the dashboard card can update instantly
  // (the form merges this into local state before the background refetch lands).
  return NextResponse.json({
```

> AGENT

Diagnosis confirmed. There is **no `FOR UPDATE` RLS policy** on `produce_listings` (only INSERT/DELETE for anon). So:

- **Edit works** → goes through `/api/farmer/update-listing` (service-role, bypasses RLS)
- **Delete works** → has an explicit anon DELETE policy
- **Pause / Suspend silently fail** → they call the **anon** client's `UPDATE`, which matches 0 rows under RLS and returns **no error**. The UI flips optimistically, then the reload reverts it — and the DB never changed, so consumers still see and can order the produce.

Let me see how Edit calls the API so I mirror it exactly.

> AGENT

All clear. Here's what I found and fixed.

## Root cause

The Pause/Suspend buttons looked like they did nothing because of an **RLS permissions mismatch**:

- **Edit** works because it goes through `/api/farmer/update-listing`, which uses the Supabase **service-role** key and bypasses RLS.
- **Delete** works because there's an explicit anon DELETE policy (`produce-listings-delete-policy.sql`).
- **Pause/Suspend** were calling the **anon** Supabase client's `.update()` directly. There is **no UPDATE RLS policy** on `produce_listings`, so PostgREST matched **0 rows and returned no error**. The UI flipped optimistically, then the background reload reverted it — and the database never changed. So consumers kept seeing and could keep ordering the produce.

The display filters themselves were already correct (consumer browse, search, region, and the public profile tab all filter to `status === 'available'`) — they just never had anything to hide because the status was never persisted.

## Changes (`src/app/farmer/dashboard/page.tsx`)

All three status toggles now route through the same service-role API that Edit uses:
- `handleToggleListingSuspend` (inline dashboard list)
- `handleTogglePause` and `handleToggleSuspend` (Manage Listings modal) — refactored through a shared `setListingStatus` helper, with proper error surfacing and re-sync on failure.

## Defense-in-depth (`src/app/api/orders/place/route.ts`)

Order placement previously fetched listings by ID with **no status check**, so a stale cart could still order a paused/suspended item. Now it rejects any listing whose status isn't `available` ("… is no longer available."). This also covers moderator-suspended produce.

Typecheck passes (`tsc --noEmit`).

One thing worth knowing: this fix works because `/api/farmer/update-listing` runs with the service-role key and verifies ownership via the signed farmer cookie — the proper long-term fix is still to add a real UPDATE RLS policy on `produce_listings`, but routing through the API matches the existing pattern and unblocks you now.

Want me to test this end-to-end with the `/verify` flow, or commit it? (I'll ask before pushing, per your standing note.)

```
import { createHmac, timingSafeEqual } from 'crypto'
import type { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
import { getSessionSecret } from '@/lib/session'

// Farmer auth cookie. Separate cookie + namespaced HMAC so a stolen
// consumer or rider token can't be replayed against farmer endpoints.
const COOKIE_NAME = 'yff_farmer'
const TOKEN_TTL_MS = 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000

function b64url(buf: Buffer): string {
  return buf.toString('base64').replace(/\+/g, '-').replace(/\//g, '_').replace(/=+$/, '')
}

function sign(payload: string): string {
  const secret=[REDACTED]()
  return b64url(createHmac('sha256', secret).update(`farmer:${payload}`).digest())
}

export function createFarmerSessionToken(farmerId: string): string {
  const issuedAt = Date.now()
  const payload = `${farmerId}.${issuedAt}`
  return `${payload}.${sign(payload)}`
}

export type FarmerSessionPayload = { farmerId: string; issuedAt: number }

export function verifyFarmerSessionToken(token: string | undefined | null): FarmerSessionPayload | null {
  if (!token) return null
  const parts = token.split('.')
  if (parts.length !== 3) return null
  const [farmerId, issuedAtStr, sig] = parts
  const issuedAt = Number(issuedAtStr)
  if (!farmerId || !Number.isFinite(issuedAt)) return null
  if (Date.now() - issuedAt > TOKEN_TTL_MS) return null

  let expected: string
  try {
    expected = sign(`${farmerId}.${issuedAtStr}`)
  } catch {
    return null
  }
  const a = Buffer.from(sig)
  const b = Buffer.from(expected)
  if (a.length !== b.length) return null
  if (!timingSafeEqual(a, b)) return null
  return { farmerId, issuedAt }
}

export function getFarmerSessionFromRequest(req: NextRequest): FarmerSessionPayload | null {
  const token = req.cookies.get(COOKIE_NAME)?.value
  return verifyFarmerSessionToken(token)
}

export function setFarmerSessionCookie(res: NextResponse, farmerId: string): void {
  const token=[REDACTED](farmerId)
  res.cookies.set(COOKIE_NAME, token, {
    httpOnly: true,
    secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production',
    sameSite: 'lax',
    path: '/',
    maxAge: Math.floor(TOKEN_TTL_MS / 1000),
  })
}

export function clearFarmerSessionCookie(res: NextResponse): void {
  res.cookies.set(COOKIE_NAME, '', {
    httpOnly: true,
    secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production',
    sameSite: 'lax',
    path: '/',
    maxAge: 0,
  })
}

export const FARMER_SESSION_COOKIE_NAME = COOKIE_NAME
```

> AGENT

Critical finding. The test exposed two things:

1. **Step 1 confirms my root-cause diagnosis exactly**: the anon UPDATE returns HTTP 200 with `rows: []` (0 rows, no error) — the silent no-op.
2. **Step 2 reveals my fix is incomplete**: routing through the service-role API now surfaces the *real* blocker — a DB **CHECK constraint** `produce_listings_status_check` rejects the value `suspended_by_farmer`. So the status values `paused`/`suspended_by_farmer` were never valid at the DB level. The feature was dead on arrival regardless of client path.

Let me confirm the constraint's allowed values.

> AGENT

Definitive root cause confirmed: the CHECK constraint **rejects `paused` and `suspended_by_farmer`** — the exact values the buttons write — while allowing `available`, `coming_soon`, `sold_out`, `suspended`. So the feature is blocked at the DB level; my code change (routing through the service-role API) is necessary but **not sufficient**. A migration is required.

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/veerendra/farmer/yourfamilyfarmer/scripts/produce-status-farmer-takedown-migration.sql",
  "content": "-- ============================================================\n-- Extend produce_listings.status CHECK to allow farmer take-downs\n-- Run this in the Supabase SQL editor (Dashboard → SQL Editor)\n-- ============================================================\n-- The farmer dashboard's Pause and Suspend buttons write the statuses\n-- 'paused' and 'suspended_by_farmer', but the existing\n-- produce_listings_status_check constraint rejected them (error 23514),\n-- so the take-downs silently never persisted and consumers could still\n-- see and order the produce. This recreates the constraint with the full\n-- set of statuses the app actually uses.\n\nALTER TABLE produce_listings\n  DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS produce_listings_status_check;\n\nALTER TABLE produce_listings\n  ADD CONSTRAINT produce_listings_status_check\n  CHECK (status IN (\n    'available',\n    'coming_soon',\n    'sold_out',\n    'pending_review',\n    'rejected',\n    'suspended',           -- moderator take-down\n    'suspended_by_farmer', -- farmer Suspend button\n    'paused'               -- farmer Pause button\n  ));\n"
}
```

> AGENT

DB restored cleanly (`available`, stock 84). Here's the verification report.

## Verification: farmer Pause/Suspend take-down of produce

**Verdict: FAIL** — the code change is correct and necessary, but **suspend still won't work in production until a DB migration is applied**. Testing surfaced a second, deeper blocker that my code fix alone doesn't resolve. I've written the migration; it must be run in the Supabase SQL editor.

**Claim:** When a farmer taps Pause/Suspend, the status must persist so consumers can no longer see or order the produce.

**Method:** Cold start (no skills). Ran `npm run dev` (Next 16, localhost:3000). Minted a valid `yff_farmer` HMAC cookie from `SESSION_SECRET`, drove the real HTTP routes against the live Supabase, and read the DB back via the REST API with the service-role key. Tested on the real "Green leaf" listing (the one from your screenshot); restored it after.

### Steps
1. ✅ **Reproduced the original bug** — anon-client `PATCH produce_listings` → HTTP **200, `rows: []`**, DB stayed `available`. The silent 0-row no-op under RLS is exactly why the buttons appeared to do nothing.
2. ❌ 🔍 **My fix exposed a hidden blocker** — suspend via the new service-role API path → HTTP **500**: `violates check constraint "produce_listings_status_check"`. The DB **rejects** the values `paused` and `suspended_by_farmer`.
3. 🔍 **Mapped the constraint** (against the real row, restored after): `available, coming_soon, sold_out, suspended` → ALLOWED; **`paused`, `suspended_by_farmer` → REJECTED (23514)**. The farmer take-down statuses were never valid at the DB level.
4. ✅ **Mechanism works once status is valid** — used `coming_soon` (an allowed status) as a proxy: API take-down → HTTP 200, DB persisted, and listing **disappeared from `/api/produce`** (consumer can't see it).
5. ✅ **Order guard works** — guest order for the taken-down listing → HTTP **400 "Green leaf is no longer available."**, and `stock_qty` stayed 84 (guard fires before any stock claim). Restored to `available`.

**Sample (the decisive captures):**
```
1. ANON update -> http 200 rows: []                 | DB: available   (silent no-op = the bug)
2. API suspend -> http 500 ...produce_listings_status_check violated  (constraint blocks it)
   paused -> REJECTED 23514 ; suspended_by_farmer -> REJECTED 23514
4. API takedown(coming_soon) -> 200 | in /api/produce: false          (consumer hidden ✅)
5. order(taken down) -> 400 {"Green leaf is no longer available."}     (order blocked ✅, stock untouched)
```

### Findings
- ⚠️ **Blocker — migration required.** `scripts/produce-status-farmer-takedown-migration.sql` (written) extends the CHECK constraint to allow `paused` and `suspended_by_farmer`. **It is not yet applied** — I can't run DDL through the REST API. Until you run it in the Supabase SQL editor, clicking Suspend will now show a 500 error instead of silently failing. The code fix + migration are a package; ship them together.
- ⚠️ **The display side was already correct all along.** Once status actually persists, `/api/produce`, search, region, and the profile tab all hide non-`available` produce — verified live in step 4. The whole bug was that the write never landed (RLS no-op + invalid status value).
- The order-placement guard is well-placed: it rejects before stock is claimed, so a stale cart can't oversell or order a taken-down item. Also covers moderator-`suspended`.
- 🔍 Confirmed the constraint only fires on rows that actually match — my first constraint probe (against a non-existent id) misleadingly showed everything "allowed"; re-running against the real row gave the true result above.

Want me to also confirm the migration once you've run it (I can re-test `paused`/`suspended_by_farmer` directly), and then commit both files? I won't push without your go-ahead.

> DEVELOPER

should i have to add any query in supabase

> AGENT

Yes — exactly one. The code fix alone won't work until you run this in Supabase, because the database currently **rejects** the `paused` and `suspended_by_farmer` status values. I already saved it to `scripts/produce-status-farmer-takedown-migration.sql`.

**Supabase Dashboard → SQL Editor → New query → paste → Run:**

```sql
ALTER TABLE produce_listings
  DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS produce_listings_status_check;

ALTER TABLE produce_listings
  ADD CONSTRAINT produce_listings_status_check
  CHECK (status IN (
    'available',
    'coming_soon',
    'sold_out',
    'pending_review',
    'rejected',
    'suspended',           -- moderator take-down
    'suspended_by_farmer', -- farmer Suspend button
    'paused'               -- farmer Pause button
  ));
```

What it does: drops the old check constraint and recreates it with `paused` and `suspended_by_farmer` added (it keeps all the existing allowed values, so nothing else breaks). It's safe to run on live data — your current rows are all `available`/`coming_soon`/etc., which stay valid.

After you run it, tell me and I'll re-test the real `paused`/`suspended_by_farmer` values end-to-end (right now I could only verify the mechanism using `coming_soon` as a proxy). Then I'll commit both the code and the migration — and ask before pushing.

If you'd rather run it from this session instead of the dashboard, type:

`! psql "<your-supabase-connection-string>" -f scripts/produce-status-farmer-takedown-migration.sql`

```
  const loadDashboard = useCallback(async () => {
    const farmerId = localStorage.getItem('yff_farmer_id')
    if (!farmerId) { router.replace('/farmer/login'); return }

    const { data: farmerData } = await supabase
      .from('farmers')
      .select('*')
      .eq('id', farmerId)
      .maybeSingle()

    if (!farmerData) { setNotFound(true); setLoading(false); return }
    setFarmer(farmerData)

    const monthStart = new Date()
    monthStart.setDate(1)
    monthStart.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0)

    const [listingsRes, pendingRes, approvedRes, intentsRes, monthlyRes] = await Promise.all([
      // Full (lightweight) listing rows so the dashboard can show them inline
      // with a quick suspend/resume; the active-listings count is derived below.
      supabase.from('produce_listings').select('id, name, emoji, status, price_tier_1_price, unit, stock_qty').eq('farmer_id', farmerData.id).order('created_at', { ascending: false }),
      // Active orders = still pending, OR approved but not yet picked up/delivered.
      // Approved orders stay here so the farmer keeps the scheduled date in view
      // until the buyer collects (or the rider delivers).
      supabase.from('orders').select('*').eq('farmer_id', farmerData.id).in('status', ['pending', 'approved']).order('created_at', { ascending: false }),
      supabase.from('orders').select('id, total_price').eq('farmer_id', farmerData.id).eq('status', 'approved').gte('created_at', new Date(Date.now() - 7 * 86400000).toISOString()),
      supabase.from('demand_intents').select('crop_name, quantity_kg').eq('region_slug', farmerData.region_slug).eq('fulfilled', false),
      supabase.from('orders').select('id, total_price, created_at').eq('farmer_id', farmerData.id).eq('status', 'approved').gte('created_at', monthStart.toISOString()),
    ])

    setListings((listingsRes.data ?? []) as DashboardListing[])
    // Drop approved orders that are already resolved (collected / delivered).
    const activeOrders = (pendingRes.data ?? []).filter((o) => !isResolved(o as Order)) as Order[]
    setPendingOrders(activeOrders)
    const approved = approvedRes.data ?? []
    setApprovedCount(approved.length)
    setTotalRevenue(approved.reduce((sum, o) => sum + (o.total_price ?? 0), 0))
```

Found the root issue. The dashboard authenticates with **`localStorage.getItem('yff_farmer_id')`** — there is **no `yff_farmer` cookie**. But `/api/farmer/update-listing` requires that cookie (`getFarmerSessionFromRequest`), so it always returns 401 "Please log in first." My test passed only because I minted the cookie by hand. Let me confirm the login flow doesn't set the cookie, and how the insert path authorizes.

> AGENT

The login page *does* call `/api/auth/login` (which sets the cookie) — but the dashboard's own auth check only reads `localStorage`. So the two can drift: `localStorage` persists forever, but the cookie can be missing or expired (e.g., you logged in before the cookie code existed, or it aged out). The dashboard still shows you as "logged in," yet the API rejects you. Let me confirm how the *working* writes (Insert/Delete) authorize, since they clearly don't need the cookie.

> AGENT

The picture is now clear. This codebase's MVP uses a **public-write model**: Insert and Delete on `produce_listings` go through the **anon client** with `USING(true)` RLS policies (see `produce-listings-delete-policy.sql`, whose comment says "anon INSERT is already allowed"). The dashboard authenticates purely via `localStorage` — there's no reliable cookie. My cookie-gated API route was the wrong call: it's inconsistent with how Insert/Delete already work, and it breaks whenever the cookie is absent (which is your case).

The clean fix: route pause/suspend through the **anon client** like Insert/Delete already do, and add the missing **UPDATE RLS policy** (the sibling of the existing DELETE policy). Let me revert the handlers and write that policy migration.

Let me see the current state of my handlers to revert precisely.

```json
  "content": "-- ============================================================\n-- RLS: allow updates on produce_listings\n-- Run this in the Supabase SQL editor (Dashboard → SQL Editor)\n-- ============================================================\n-- The farmer dashboard needs to update a farmer's own produce — most\n-- visibly the Pause and Suspend buttons (status changes), but also\n-- inline edits. There was no UPDATE policy, so the anon client's\n-- .update() matched 0 rows and silently persisted nothing.\n-- Matches the public-write model used elsewhere in the MVP (anon\n-- INSERT and DELETE are already allowed). Tighten with auth.uid()\n-- once farmer auth is wired up.\n\nDROP POLICY IF EXISTS \"produce_listings public update\" ON produce_listings;\nCREATE POLICY \"produce_listings public update\"\n  ON produce_listings FOR UPDATE\n  USING (true)\n  WITH CHECK (true);\n"
}
```

> AGENT

Confirmed — typecheck passes, and the anon UPDATE currently returns `200` with **0 rows changed** (blocked, exactly the silent no-op). One SQL statement fixes it.

## What was wrong with my first attempt

I routed pause/suspend through `/api/farmer/update-listing`, which requires the `yff_farmer` **cookie**. But your dashboard logs in via **`localStorage` only** — the cookie isn't reliably present — so the API returned **401 "Please log in first."** (Edit uses that same route, so it has the same flaw.) My earlier test passed only because I forged the cookie by hand. My mistake.

## The clean fix (now in place)

Pause/Suspend now use the **anon Supabase client**, exactly like the Add and Delete produce flows already do — no cookie, works with your `localStorage` login. The only missing piece is the matching RLS **UPDATE** policy (Insert and Delete already have theirs).

**Run this in Supabase → SQL Editor** (saved as `scripts/produce-listings-update-policy.sql`):

```sql
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "produce_listings public update" ON produce_listings;
CREATE POLICY "produce_listings public update"
  ON produce_listings FOR UPDATE
  USING (true)
  WITH CHECK (true);
```

Once you've run it, tell me — I'll verify the anon update now persists `paused`/`suspended_by_farmer` end-to-end (and that consumers can't see/order), then commit and push.

Note: the earlier CHECK-constraint migration you already ran is still needed and correct — keep it. This policy is the second, final piece.

Use `.select('id')` after `.update()`/`.delete()` to detect the silent 0-row no-op when a policy is missing. Note: Edit still uses the cookie-gated route, so it likely has the same latent flaw.\n"
```json
}
```

```
=== moderator consumers API (PATCH) ===
src/app/api/moderator/consumers/route.ts
src/app/api/moderator/consumers/[id]/route.ts
---
import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
import { isModeratorRequest, getModeratorZone } from '@/lib/moderator-session'

export const runtime = 'nodejs'
export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'

const UUID_RE = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i

function svc() {
  return createClient(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,
  )
}

// PATCH — suspend or reactivate a consumer account.
//   { suspended: true | false }
// A moderator may only act on a consumer who has ordered from a farmer in their
// zone, so one zone's moderator can't touch another zone's buyers.
export async function PATCH(req: NextRequest, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  if (!isModeratorRequest(req)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Moderator login required.' }, { status: 401 })
  }
  const { id } = await params
  if (!id || !UUID_RE.test(id)) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid consumer id.' }, { status: 400 })

  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null)
  const suspended = (body as { suspended?: unknown })?.suspended
  if (typeof suspended !== 'boolean') {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'suspended must be true or false.' }, { status: 400 })
  }
  // A reason is mandatory when suspending so the buyer (and other moderators)
  // can see why. Cleared automatically on reactivation.
  const reasonRaw = (body as { reason?: unknown })?.reason
  const reason = typeof reasonRaw === 'string' ? reasonRaw.trim() : ''
  if (suspended && !reason) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'A reason is required to suspend an account.' }, { status: 400 })
  }

  const zone = getModeratorZone(req)
  const supabase = svc()

  // Confirm the consumer exists.
  const { data: consumer } = await supabase
    .from('consumers_auth').select('id').eq('id', id).maybeSingle()
  if (!consumer) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Consumer not found.' }, { status: 404 })

  // Zone check: the consumer must have at least one order from a farmer here.
  const { data: zoneFarmers } = await supabase.from('farmers').select('id').eq('region_slug', zone)
  const farmerIds = (zoneFarmers ?? []).map((f) => f.id)
  let inZone = false
  if (farmerIds.length > 0) {
    const { count } = await supabase
      .from('orders')
      .select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true })
      .eq('consumer_id', id)
      .in('farmer_id', farmerIds)
    inZone = (count ?? 0) > 0
  }
  if (!inZone) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'This consumer has not ordered in your zone.' }, { status: 403 })
  }

  const { error } = await supabase
    .from('consumers_auth')
    .update({
      suspended,
      suspended_at: suspended ? new Date().toISOString() : null,
      suspended_reason: suspended ? reason : null,
    })
    .eq('id', id)
  if (error) {
    console.error('[YFF moderator/consumers PATCH] update failed:', error.message)
    return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 500 })
  }
  return NextResponse.json({ id, suspended, suspended_reason: suspended ? reason : null })
}
```

> AGENT

I checked the code, and here's what I found: **this feature is already fully built and wired end-to-end.** Here's my understanding of your request mapped to what already exists:

**1. Reason while suspending (moderator side)** — ✅ exists
`src/app/moderator/consumers/page.tsx` prompts the moderator for a reason when they hit Suspend, and it's **mandatory** (blocks if empty):
> "Reason for suspending {name}? సస్పెండ్ చేయడానికి కారణం? (e.g. Multiple fake orders)"

It's sent to `PATCH /api/moderator/consumers/[id]`, which saves it to the `consumers_auth.suspended_reason` column (I confirmed that column **exists** in your Supabase). Reactivating clears it.

**2. Shown in the consumer header, highlighted** — ✅ exists
`src/components/consumer/GlobalNav.tsx` renders a **red banner** (🚫, `bg-red-600`) at the top:
> **ACCOUNT SUSPENDED / ఖాతా నిలిపివేయబడింది**
> _{the moderator's reason}_

It's driven by `suspendedReason` from `ConsumerAuthContext`, fed by `/api/consumer/me`, and cached in `localStorage` so it survives the session being cleared.

---

So the code for exactly what you described is already there. One nuance worth knowing: a suspended consumer is **blocked at login** (`/api/consumer/login` rejects them with the reason in the error message), so the **header banner** mainly shows for someone who gets suspended *mid-session*. A freshly-suspended person logging in sees the error message, not the header banner.

Given that the last two features you reported also *looked* done but were actually broken at the DB/RLS layer — **do you want me to verify this one actually works end-to-end** (simulate a suspended consumer and confirm the banner renders)? Or are you seeing it fail / wanting a change to how it behaves (e.g. show the banner even on a blocked login attempt)?

```
=== /api/consumer/me full ===
import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
import { getConsumerSessionFromRequest, clearSessionCookie } from '@/lib/session'

export const runtime = 'nodejs'
export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  const session = getConsumerSessionFromRequest(req)
  if (!session) {
    return NextResponse.json({ consumer: null }, { status: 200 })
  }

  const supabase = createClient(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,
  )

  const { data: user } = await supabase
    .from('consumers_auth')
    .select('id, name, phone, suspended, suspended_reason')
    .eq('id', session.consumerId)
    .maybeSingle()

  if (!user) {
    // Cookie references a deleted account — clear it so the client logs out.
    const res = NextResponse.json({ consumer: null }, { status: 200 })
    clearSessionCookie(res)
    return res
  }

  if (user.suspended === true) {
    // Suspended account: clear the cookie so they can't perform authenticated
    // actions. The moderator's reason IS returned to the consumer so the header
    // banner can show why they were suspended (per client request). Falls back
    // to null for legacy suspensions with no recorded reason, in which case the
    // client shows a generic message.
    const res = NextResponse.json(
      { consumer: null, suspended: true, suspendedReason: user.suspended_reason ?? null },
      { status: 200 },
    )
    clearSessionCookie(res)
    return res
  }

  return NextResponse.json({ consumer: { id: user.id, name: user.name, phone: user.phone } })
}
```

```
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'

type DeliveryStatus = 'unassigned' | 'assigned' | 'picked_up' | 'out_for_delivery' | 'delivered'

// Fire a browser notification for a buyer-facing status change (e.g. the farmer
// shipped the parcel). Permission-gated: silently no-ops unless the buyer has
// granted notifications, so it's safe to call unconditionally.
function fireConsumerNotification(title: string, body: string) {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined' || !('Notification' in window)) return
  if (Notification.permission !== 'granted') return
  try {
    new Notification(title, { body, icon: '/icon-192.png', tag: 'yff-order-status' })
  } catch { /* some browsers throw on background tabs — ignore */ }
}

  quantity: number | null
  unit: string | null
```

I've now traced the whole suspension flow, and I want to make sure I fix the right thing rather than guess. Here's what I found:

- The suspension **does** already show as a highlighted in-app banner (red bar in `GlobalNav`) — **not** as a browser/OS notification. The only `new Notification()` in the consumer app is for *order status* updates, unrelated to suspension.
- **But** there's a real gap: a suspended user is **blocked at login**, so a freshly-suspended person never reaches the app — they only see an error message inside the **login popup** (AuthModal). The header banner only appears for someone who was *already logged in* when suspended.

So when you say "they have to see it in the user UI only, not a browser notification," I want to pin down exactly what you're seeing and want.

```
      credentials: 'same-origin',
      body: JSON.stringify({ phone, password }),
    })

      return { ok: false, error: json?.error ?? 'Login failed.' }
    }
    handleAuthSuccess(json.consumer)
    return { ok: true }
  }, [handleAuthSuccess])

  const register = useCallback(async (payload: RegisterPayload): Promise<AuthResult> => {
    const r = await fetch('/api/consumer/register', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      credentials: 'same-origin',
      body: JSON.stringify(payload),
    })
    const json = await r.json().catch(() => ({}))
    if (!r.ok || !json?.ok || !json?.consumer) {
      return { ok: false, error: json?.error ?? 'Sign-up failed.' }
    }
    handleAuthSuccess(json.consumer)
    return { ok: true }
  }, [handleAuthSuccess])

  const logout = useCallback(async () => {
    await fetch('/api/consumer/logout', { method: 'POST', credentials: 'same-origin' }).catch(() => null)
    writeCachedConsumer(null)
    setState({ status: 'anonymous', consumer: null })
  }, [])

    if (state.status === 'authenticated') {
      action()
      return
    }
    pendingAction.current = action
    setModalOpen(true)
  }, [state.status])
```

```
77	                mode === 'login' ? 'bg-white text-green-800 shadow-sm' : 'text-gray-500'
78	              }`}
```

```
=== LanguageContext.tsx ===
'use client'

import { createContext, useContext, useState, useEffect, ReactNode } from 'react'
import { t, Language, TranslationKey } from './translations'

type LanguageContextType = {
  lang: Language
  setLang: (lang: Language) => void
  tx: typeof t.en
  bi: (key: TranslationKey) => string
  // Pick the active language from an explicit English/Telugu pair. Used to
  // convert inline bilingual UI ("English / తెలుగు") into text that actually
  // switches with the toggle, without fragile string-splitting.
  L: (en: string, te: string) => string
}

const LanguageContext = createContext<LanguageContextType>({
  lang: 'en',
  setLang: () => {},
  tx: t.en,
  bi: (key) => t.en[key] ?? '',
  L: (en) => en,
})

export function LanguageProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [lang, setLangState] = useState<Language>('en')

  useEffect(() => {
    const saved = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? localStorage.getItem('yff_lang') : null
    if (saved === 'en' || saved === 'te') setLangState(saved)
  }, [])

  const setLang = (next: Language) => {
    setLangState(next)
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') localStorage.setItem('yff_lang', next)
  }

  const tx = t[lang]
  const bi = (key: TranslationKey) => (t[lang] as Record<string, string>)[key] ?? t.en[key] ?? ''
  const L = (en: string, te: string) => (lang === 'te' ? te : en)

  return (
    <LanguageContext.Provider value={{ lang, setLang, tx, bi, L }}>
      {children}
    </LanguageContext.Provider>
  )
}

export function useLang() {
  return useContext(LanguageContext)
}
```

```json
{
  "command": "cd /home/veerendra/farmer/yourfamilyfarmer\ncat > /tmp/i18n_codemod.py <<'PYEOF'\nimport re, sys\n\nTE = r'ఀ-౿'\nhas_te = re.compile(f'[{TE}]')\n\ndef esc(s):\n    return s.replace('\\\\', '\\\\\\\\').replace(\"'\", \"\\\\'\")\n\ndef make_L(en, te):\n    return f\"L('{esc(en.strip())}', '{esc(te.strip())}')\"\n\ndef split_bi(content):\n    # Only handle a single ' / ' split with Telugu on the right side.\n    parts = content.split(' / ')\n    if len(parts) != 2:\n        return None\n    en, te = parts\n    if not has_te.search(te) or has_te.search(en):\n        return None\n    if not en.strip():\n        return None\n    return en, te\n\ndef process(text):\n    skipped = []\n    out = []\n    i = 0\n    n = len(text)\n    # Tokenize-ish line by line to keep it simple and safe.\n    lines = text.split('\\n')\n    new_lines = []\n    for lineno, line in enumerate(lines, 1):\n        if ' / ' not in line or not has_te.search(line):\n            new_lines.append(line); continue\n        original = line\n\n        # Case 1: JSX attribute  attr=\"A / తె\"  or attr='A / తె'\n        def attr_repl(m):\n            q = m.group('q'); content = m.group('c')\n            sp = split_bi(content)\n            if not sp: return m.group(0)\n            return f\"{m.group('pre')}={{{make_L(*sp)}}}\"\n        line = re.sub(r\"(?P<pre>\\b[\\w-]+)=(?P<q>[\\\"'])(?P<c>[^\\\"']*? / [^\\\"']*?)(?P=q)\", attr_repl, line)\n\n        # Case 2: plain quoted JS string  '...A / తె...'  or \"...\"\n        def str_repl(m):\n            q = m.group('q'); content = m.group('c')\n            sp = split_bi(content)\n            if not sp: return m.group(0)\n            return make_L(*sp)\n        line = re.sub(r\"(?P<q>[\\\"'])(?P<c>[^\\\"']*? / [^\\\"']*?)(?P=q)\", str_repl, line)\n\n        # Case 3: pure JSX text node  >A / తె<   (no braces/tags inside)\n        def jsx_repl(m):\n            content = m.group('c')\n            sp = split_bi(content)\n            if not sp: return m.group(0)\n            return f\">{{{make_L(*sp)}}}<\"\n        line = re.sub(r\">(?P<c>[^<>{}]*? / [^<>{}]*?)<\", jsx_repl, line)\n\n        if line == original and ' / ' in original and has_te.search(original):\n            skipped.append((lineno, original.strip()))\n        new_lines.append(line)\n    return '\\n'.join(new_lines), skipped\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    path = sys.argv[1]\n    apply = '--apply' in sys.argv\n    src = open(path, encoding='utf-8').read()\n    new, skipped = process(src)\n    if apply:\n        open(path, 'w', encoding='utf-8').write(new)\n        print(f\"APPLIED {path}\")\n    else:\n        print(f\"--- DRY RUN {path} ---\")\n        # show diff-ish\n        for a,b in zip(src.split('\\n'), new.split('\\n')):\n            if a!=b:\n                print(f\"-  {a.strip()}\")\n                print(f\"+  {b.strip()}\")\n    if skipped:\n        print(f\"  [SKIPPED {len(skipped)} complex lines in {path}]\")\n        for ln, s in skipped:\n            print(f\"    L{ln}: {s[:110]}\")\nPYEOF\npython3 /tmp/i18n_codemod.py src/app/farmer/login/page.tsx 2>&1 | head -60",
  "description": "Write codemod and dry-run on farmer login"
}
```

```json
{
  "command": "cd /home/veerendra/farmer/yourfamilyfarmer\ncat > /tmp/wire.py <<'PYEOF'\nimport re, sys\nfiles = sys.argv[1:]\nfor path in files:\n    s = open(path, encoding='utf-8').read()\n    lines = s.split('\\n')\n\n    # 1. ensure 'use client'\n    if not lines[0].strip().startswith(\"'use client'\") and not lines[0].strip().startswith('\"use client\"'):\n        lines = [\"'use client'\", \"\"] + lines\n\n    # 2. add useLang import if missing\n    text = '\\n'.join(lines)\n    if 'useLang' not in text:\n        # insert after last top-of-file import line\n        last_imp = -1\n        for idx, ln in enumerate(lines[:60]):\n            if ln.startswith('import '):\n                last_imp = idx\n        imp = \"import { useLang } from '@/lib/LanguageContext'\"\n        if last_imp >= 0:\n            lines.insert(last_imp+1, imp)\n        else:\n            # after use client (line 0/1)\n            ins = 1\n            lines.insert(ins, imp); lines.insert(ins, \"\")\n\n    # 3. insert hook after component body opening brace\n    text = '\\n'.join(lines)\n    if not re.search(r'\\{[^}\\n]*\\bL\\b[^}\\n]*\\}\\s*=\\s*useLang\\(\\)', text):\n        # find export default function\n        start = None\n        for idx, ln in enumerate(lines):\n            if re.search(r'export default function', ln):\n                start = idx; break\n        if start is not None:\n            depth = 0; seen_paren = False; insert_at = None\n            for idx in range(start, len(lines)):\n                ln = lines[idx]\n                depth += ln.count('(') - ln.count(')')\n                if '(' in ln: seen_paren = True\n                if seen_paren and depth == 0 and ln.rstrip().endswith('{'):\n                    insert_at = idx; break\n            if insert_at is not None:\n                indent = '  '\n                lines.insert(insert_at+1, f\"{indent}const {{ L }} = useLang()\")\n    open(path, 'w', encoding='utf-8').write('\\n'.join(lines))\n    print(\"wired\", path)\nPYEOF\npython3 /tmp/wire.py \\\n  src/app/admin/login/page.tsx src/app/admin/page.tsx src/app/buyer-protection/page.tsx \\\n  src/app/consumer/complaints/page.tsx src/app/farmer/complaints/page.tsx src/app/farmer/login/page.tsx \\\n  src/app/farmer/signup/page.tsx src/app/moderator/login/page.tsx src/app/not-found.tsx \\\n  src/app/rider/complaints/page.tsx src/app/rider/dashboard/page.tsx src/app/rider/login/page.tsx \\\n  src/app/rider/signup/page.tsx src/components/LocationSearch.tsx src/components/consumer/MyOrdersChip.tsx\necho \"--- skipped (manual): error.tsx (GlobalError props), ForgotPasswordModal, AuthModal, ComplaintModal ---\"",
```

```
===== src/app/error.tsx =====
'use client'

import { useEffect } from 'react'
import Link from 'next/link'

export default function GlobalError({
  error,
  reset,
}: {
  error: Error & { digest?: string }
  reset: () => void
}) {
  useEffect(() => {
    console.error('[YFF page error]', error.message)
  }, [error])

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-gray-50 flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-6 text-center">
      <div className="text-6xl mb-4">⚠️</div>
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-gray-900">Something went wrong</h1>
      <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 mt-1">ఏదో తప్పు జరిగింది. మళ్ళీ ప్రయత్నించండి.</p>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 mt-6 w-full max-w-xs">
        <button
          onClick={() => reset()}
          className="bg-green-700 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm active:bg-green-800"
        >
          {L('Try again', 'మళ్ళీ ప్రయత్నించండి')}
        </button>
        <Link
          href="/"
          className="border border-gray-300 text-gray-700 font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm"
        >
          Go to home
        </Link>
      </div>
    </main>
  )
}

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import type { UserType } from '@/lib/otp-accounts'

type Step = 'phone' | 'otp' | 'password' | 'done'

const OTP_LEN = 6
const OTP_WINDOW_S = 10 * 60 // 10 minutes
const RESEND_AFTER_S = 60

function maskPhone(p: string) {
  const d = p.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(-10)
  if (d.length !== 10) return p
  return `+91 XXXXXX${d.slice(6)}`
}

function fmt(s: number) {
  const m = Math.floor(s / 60)
  const r = s % 60
  return `${m}:${String(r).padStart(2, '0')}`
}

export default function ForgotPasswordModal({
  userType,
  initialPhone = '',
  onClose,
  onResetComplete,
}: {
  userType: UserType

  onResetComplete?: () => void
}) {

      credentials: 'same-origin',
```

id: toolu_01H1js2wid7Z2aHhcoep4GaL
```json
{
  "command": "cd /home/veerendra/farmer/yourfamilyfarmer\npython3 - <<'PYEOF'\nimport re, glob\nfiles = set(glob.glob('src/**/*.tsx', recursive=True) + glob.glob('src/**/*.ts', recursive=True))\nchanged = 0\nfor path in files:\n    s = open(path, encoding='utf-8').read()\n    if 'useLang()' not in s: continue\n    def repl(m):\n        inner = m.group(1)\n        names = [x.strip() for x in inner.split(',') if x.strip()]\n        if 'L' in names: return m.group(0)\n        names.append('L')\n        return 'const { ' + ', '.join(names) + ' } = useLang()'\n    new = re.sub(r'const \\{([^}]*)\\} = useLang\\(\\)', repl, s)\n    if new != s:\n        open(path,'w',encoding='utf-8').write(new); changed += 1\n        print(\"added L ->\", path)\nprint(\"files changed:\", changed)\nPYEOF\necho \"=== re-typecheck ===\"\nnpx tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json 2>&1 | head -40",
  "description": "Add L to all useLang destructures, re-typecheck"
}
```

All 170 remaining errors are "Cannot find name 'L'" — in separately-defined sub-components (PascalCase functions) and a few module-scope constants. Let me write an inserter that adds the hook to each PascalCase component function that uses `L` but lacks the hook.

> TOOL

tool_use Bash
id: toolu_013nrYP4HvqZrrfautH5jghG
```json
{
  "command": "cd /home/veerendra/farmer/yourfamilyfarmer\ncat > /tmp/addhook.py <<'PYEOF'\nimport re, sys\n\ndef find_component_bodies(lines):\n    \"\"\"Yield (open_brace_line_idx) for each PascalCase function/const-arrow component.\"\"\"\n    starts = []\n    for i, ln in enumerate(lines):\n        if re.search(r'\\bfunction [A-Z]\\w*\\s*\\(', ln) or re.search(r'\\bconst [A-Z]\\w*\\s*=\\s*\\(', ln) or re.search(r'\\bconst [A-Z]\\w*:\\s*React\\.', ln):\n            starts.append(i)\n    return starts\n\ndef body_range(lines, start):\n    # find opening brace of body (paren depth back to 0, line ends with {)\n    depth = 0; seen = False\n    for i in range(start, min(start+40, len(lines))):\n        depth += lines[i].count('(') - lines[i].count(')')\n        if '(' in lines[i]: seen = True\n        if seen and depth <= 0 and lines[i].rstrip().endswith('{'):\n            open_idx = i\n            break\n    else:\n        return None\n    # find matching close brace\n    bdepth = 0; started=False\n    for j in range(open_idx, len(lines)):\n        bdepth += lines[j].count('{') - lines[j].count('}')\n        if lines[j].count('{')>0: started=True\n        if started and bdepth == 0:\n            return (open_idx, j)\n    return (open_idx, len(lines)-1)\n\nfor path in sys.argv[1:]:\n    s = open(path, encoding='utf-8').read()\n    lines = s.split('\\n')\n    inserts = []  # (after_idx, indent)\n    for st in find_component_bodies(lines):\n        rng = body_range(lines, st)\n        if not rng: continue\n        o, c = rng\n        body = '\\n'.join(lines[o:c+1])\n        if 'L(' in body and '= useLang()' not in body:\n            indent = re.match(r'\\s*', lines[o+1] if o+1 < len(lines) else '  ').group(0) or '  '\n            inserts.append((o, indent))\n    # apply bottom-up\n    for o, indent in sorted(set(inserts), reverse=True):\n        lines.insert(o+1, f\"{indent}const {{ L }} = useLang()\")\n    if inserts:\n        open(path,'w',encoding='utf-8').write('\\n'.join(lines))\n        print(f\"{path}: inserted {len(inserts)} hooks\")\nPYEOF\npython3 /tmp/addhook.py $(npx tsc --noEmit 2>&1 | grep -oE \"^[^(]+\" | sort -u)\necho \"=== re-typecheck count ===\"\nnpx tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json 2>&1 | grep -c \"Cannot find name 'L'\"",
  "description": "Auto-insert hooks into sub-components"
}
```

```json
{
  "command": "cd /home/veerendra/farmer/yourfamilyfarmer\ncat > /tmp/addhook2.py <<'PYEOF'\nimport re, sys\n\nSTART_RE = re.compile(r'^(export\\s+)?(default\\s+)?function\\s+[A-Z]\\w*\\s*\\(|^(export\\s+)?const\\s+[A-Z]\\w*\\s*(:[^=]+)?=\\s*\\(')\n\ndef open_idx_of(lines, start):\n    depth = 0; seen = False\n    for i in range(start, min(start+60, len(lines))):\n        depth += lines[i].count('(') - lines[i].count(')')\n        if '(' in lines[i]: seen = True\n        if seen and depth <= 0 and lines[i].rstrip().endswith('{'):\n            return i\n    return None\n\nfor path in sys.argv[1:]:\n    lines = open(path, encoding='utf-8').read().split('\\n')\n    starts = [i for i,ln in enumerate(lines) if START_RE.search(ln)]\n    starts_sorted = sorted(starts)\n    inserts = []\n    for idx, st in enumerate(starts_sorted):\n        o = open_idx_of(lines, st)\n        if o is None: continue\n        nxt = starts_sorted[idx+1] if idx+1 < len(starts_sorted) else len(lines)\n        body = '\\n'.join(lines[o:nxt])\n        if 'L(' in body and '= useLang()' not in body:\n            indent = (re.match(r'\\s*', lines[o+1]).group(0) if o+1 < len(lines) else '  ') or '  '\n            inserts.append((o, indent))\n    for o, indent in sorted(set(inserts), reverse=True):\n        lines.insert(o+1, f\"{indent}const {{ L }} = useLang()\")\n    if inserts:\n        open(path,'w',encoding='utf-8').write('\\n'.join(lines))\n        print(f\"{path}: +{len(inserts)} hooks\")\nPYEOF\npython3 /tmp/addhook2.py $(npx tsc --noEmit 2>&1 | grep -oE \"^[^(]+\" | sort -u)\necho \"=== remaining L errors ===\"\nnpx tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json 2>&1 | grep -c \"Cannot find name 'L'\"",
  "description": "Rewrite detector, re-run, recount"
}
```

```json
{
  "command": "cd /home/veerendra/farmer/yourfamilyfarmer\ncat > /tmp/brmerge.py <<'PYEOF'\nimport re, glob\nTE = 'ఀ-౿'\nte_re = re.compile(f'[{TE}]')\ndef esc(s): return s.replace('\\\\','\\\\\\\\').replace(\"'\", \"\\\\'\")\nfiles = glob.glob('src/**/*.tsx', recursive=True)\ntotal=0\nfor path in files:\n    lines = open(path,encoding='utf-8').read().split('\\n')\n    out=[]; i=0; n=len(lines); changed=False\n    while i < n:\n        a = lines[i]; at = a.strip()\n        m = re.match(r'^([^<>{}]+?)<br ?/?>$', at)\n        if m and not te_re.search(at) and i+1 < n:\n            b = lines[i+1]; bt = b.strip()\n            if te_re.search(bt) and not re.search(r'[<>{}]', bt):\n                indent = a[:len(a)-len(a.lstrip())]\n                out.append(f\"{indent}{{L('{esc(m.group(1).strip())}', '{esc(bt)}')}}\")\n                i += 2; changed=True; total+=1; continue\n        out.append(a); i+=1\n    if changed:\n        open(path,'w',encoding='utf-8').write('\\n'.join(out))\n        print(\"merged in\", path)\nprint(\"total br-merges:\", total)\nPYEOF\npython3 /tmp/brmerge.py",
  "description": "Merge English-br-Telugu pairs app-wide"
}
```

```
'use client'

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'

const ROLE_KEY = 'yff_role'

export default function RoleGateModal() {
  const router = useRouter()
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)

  // Mount: open the modal if the visitor hasn't picked a role yet. Done in a
  // useEffect so SSR doesn't render anything (avoids hydration mismatch).
  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      const existing = localStorage.getItem(ROLE_KEY)
      if (!existing) setOpen(true)
    } catch {
      // localStorage might be blocked in private modes — just skip the gate.
    }
```

```json
{
  "command": "cd /home/veerendra/farmer/yourfamilyfarmer\ncat > /tmp/sibmerge.py <<'PYEOF'\nimport re, glob\nTE='ఀ-౿'; te=re.compile(f'[{TE}]')\ndef esc(s): return s.replace('\\\\','\\\\\\\\').replace(\"'\", \"\\\\'\")\n# element line: <tag attrs>inner</tag>\nEL=re.compile(r'^(<(\\w+)(?:\\s[^>]*)?>)(.*)(</\\2>)$')\nfiles=glob.glob('src/**/*.tsx',recursive=True)\ntot=0\nfor path in files:\n    lines=open(path,encoding='utf-8').read().split('\\n')\n    out=[];i=0;n=len(lines);ch=False\n    while i<n:\n        a=lines[i]; at=a.strip()\n        ma=EL.match(at)\n        if ma and not te.search(ma.group(3)) and re.search(r'[A-Za-z]',ma.group(3)) and '{' not in ma.group(3) and i+1<n:\n            b=lines[i+1].strip(); mb=EL.match(b)\n            if mb and te.search(mb.group(3)) and '{' not in mb.group(3) and '<' not in mb.group(3)[:-len(mb.group(4))] :\n                indent=a[:len(a)-len(a.lstrip())]\n                merged=f\"{indent}{ma.group(1)}{{L('{esc(ma.group(3).strip())}', '{esc(mb.group(3).strip())}')}}{ma.group(4)}\"\n                out.append(merged); i+=2; ch=True; tot+=1; continue\n        out.append(a); i+=1\n    if ch:\n        open(path,'w',encoding='utf-8').write('\\n'.join(out)); print(\"sib-merged\",path)\nprint(\"total sib merges:\",tot)\nPYEOF\npython3 /tmp/sibmerge.py\necho \"=== typecheck errors: $(npx tsc --noEmit 2>&1 | grep -c 'error TS') ===\"",
  "description": "Generic sibling-pair merge"
}
```

```
    // and tagged <html> when the splash had played before this session.
    const alreadyShown = document.documentElement.classList.contains('splash-skip')

    if (alreadyShown) {
      setPhase('done')
      return
    }

    timers.current.push(setTimeout(() => setPhase('out'), 2900))  // 2.9s: start fade out
    timers.current.push(setTimeout(() => setPhase('done'), 3400)) // 3.4s: home visible

    return clearTimers
  }, [])

  // Tapping anywhere during the splash skips it immediately.
  const skip = () => {
    clearTimers()
    setPhase('done')
  }
```

```
// everything except .yff-receipt so "Print / Save as PDF" produces a clean
// one-page receipt on both phones and laptops.
function ReceiptOverlay({ order, onClose }: { order: Order; onClose: () => void }) {
  const { lang, L } = useLang()
  const placed = new Date(order.created_at).toLocaleString('en-IN', {
    day: 'numeric', month: 'short', year: 'numeric', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit',
  })
  const paymentRef = order.razorpay_payment_id || order.order_code || order.id

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 overflow-y-auto">
      <style>{`@media print {
        body * { visibility: hidden !important; }
        .yff-receipt, .yff-receipt * { visibility: visible !important; }
        .yff-receipt { position: absolute; left: 0; top: 0; width: 100%; box-shadow: none !important; }
        .yff-no-print { display: none !important; }
      }`}</style>

      <div className="yff-receipt bg-white rounded-2xl w-full max-w-sm p-6 my-auto">
        <div className="text-center border-b border-dashed border-gray-300 pb-3">
          <p className="text-lg font-extrabold text-green-700">YourFamilyFarmer</p>
          <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-500">{L('Payment Receipt', 'చెల్లింపు రసీదు')}</p>
        </div>
```

```
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'

// Phases: 'show' while visible, 'out' during the fade, 'done' = unmounted.

// paints on the very first frame — covering the page so the consumer screen
```

```json
{
```

```json
  "content": "import type { NextRequest } from 'next/server'\n\nexport type Lang = 'en' | 'te'\n\n// Read the user's chosen language from the `yff_lang` cookie set by the client\n// LanguageProvider. Defaults to English. Lets API routes localise the error\n// messages they return so the UI shows only one language at a time.\nexport function reqLang(req: NextRequest): Lang {\n  return req.cookies.get('yff_lang')?.value === 'te' ? 'te' : 'en'\n}\n\nexport function tr(lang: Lang, en: string, te: string): string {\n  return lang === 'te' ? te : en\n}\n"
}
```

```
1	import { Language } from './translations'
2	
3	/**
4	 * Produce names and varieties are stored bilingually as "English / తెలుగు".
5	 * Show only the side that matches the active language. When there's no "/"
6	 * separator we return the whole string unchanged, so single-language values
7	 * (and names that happen to contain no slash) are left alone.
8	 */
9	export function localizeName(value: string | null | undefined, lang: Language): string {
10	  if (!value) return ''
11	  const sepIdx = value.indexOf('/')
12	  if (sepIdx === -1) return value.trim()
13	  const en = value.slice(0, sepIdx).trim()
14	  const te = value.slice(sepIdx + 1).trim()
15	  return lang === 'te' ? te || en : en || te
16	}
17	
```

The whole-app language separation is complete. Here's the summary.

## What changed

**The problem:** the app showed both languages at once in several formats — `"All / అన్నీ"` (slash), English heading + separate Telugu subtitle, `English<br/>Telugu`, and `{en}/{te}` data fields.

**The approach:** I used the existing `L(en, te)` helper from `LanguageContext` (which returns one side based on the toggle) and converted every bilingual display site to it. This was a large, verified codemod across **53 files** + 2 new files.

**Coverage (all done, build + typecheck clean):**
- **488 inline `"English / తెలుగు"`** strings → `L('English', 'తెలుగు')` (JSX text, attributes, JS strings, template literals)
- **Separate-element pairs** (heading+subtitle, `<br/>` splits) → merged into single `L()` calls
- **Module-scope option/label arrays** (`TYPE_OPTIONS`, `STATUS_LABEL`, `VEHICLE_OPTIONS`, `UNIT_OPTIONS`) → converted to `(L) => [...]` factories so they localize reactively
- **Data arrays** (`CATEGORIES`, buyer-protection cards) → rendered with `L(x.en, x.te)`
- **`alert`/`confirm`/`prompt`** dialogs → `L()`
- **5 API routes** (login, OTP, reset, consumer-login) → `tr(lang, en, te)`, reading a new `yff_lang` **cookie**
- Every screen: navbar, tabs, buttons, filters, dropdowns, product cards, cart, complaints, profile, forms, error messages, popups, splash, role gate, 404/error pages, admin/moderator/rider/farmer/consumer areas

**Persistence:** already worked via `localStorage` (`yff_lang`); I added a mirrored cookie so server-rendered API errors also respect the choice. Survives refresh and navigation.

**Two structural changes worth noting:**
- `buyer-protection` was a server component exporting `metadata`; I split it into a server page + `BuyerProtectionContent` client component (build blocker otherwise).
- New files: `src/lib/serverLang.ts` (server `reqLang`/`tr` helpers) and `BuyerProtectionContent.tsx`.

**Verification:**
- `tsc --noEmit`: **0 errors**
- `npm run build`: **succeeds**, all routes compile
- Runtime smoke test: `/consumer` and other routes return 200; default render is English-only — **no `All / అన్నీ`**, no stray `English / Telugu` anywhere. The only remaining `/` with Telugu is a code comment in `localizeName.ts`.
- `localizeName` (DB-stored bilingual produce names) already splits by language — confirmed.

One caveat I can't fully automate-verify: I confirmed the **English** default render and the `L()` logic is trivial (`lang === 'te' ? te : en`), but I didn't click the toggle in a real browser to watch Telugu render live. If you want, I can drive it with a headless browser. Otherwise this is ready.

Want me to commit and push? (53 modified + 2 new files; on `main`, I'll confirm before pushing.)
